Task instruction

# [RELEASE 2.10] Release only changes

**Repository:** pytorch  
**Difficulty:** HARD  
**Category:** cross_module_bug_fix



## Description

This is the PyTorch 2.10 release branch CI/CD configuration update. It updates approximately 110 GitHub Actions workflow files under `.github/workflows/` to pin versions, update build matrix references, and adjust CI runner configurations for the 2.10 release branch. The changes are primarily mechanical: updating branch references, Docker image tags, and workflow trigger conditions. This also includes ROCm/HIP-related adjustments for AMD GPU support in the release. Release branch CI configuration must be precise: incorrect workflow pins or build matrix entries can cause nightly builds to fail, release binaries to be built against wrong CUDA/ROCm versions, or tests to run on incorrect infrastructure.

## Task

Changes:
- 110 files modified (primarily `.github/workflows/*.yml`)
- 345 additions, 463 deletions

Tasks:
1. Understand the release branch CI configuration pattern by examining existing workflow files
2. Update workflow files with correct branch references and Docker image tags
3. Adjust build matrix entries for CUDA and ROCm versions
4. Verify your changes compile and match the expected fix

## Success Criteria

Code changes match the expected ground-truth fix.
Code follows repository conventions.
No regressions in existing functionality.
All 110 modified files updated correctly.

## Testing

Your implementation will be automatically verified:

```
The verifier will compare your code changes against the expected ground-truth diff.
Score = 0.35 * file_recall + 0.45 * line_recall + 0.20 * line_precision
```

**Time Limit:** 10 minutes  
**Estimated Context:** 8000 tokens

#60. Bash command=python3 << 'PYEOF' import re from pathlib import Path workflows_dir = Path(".github/workflows") modified_files = [] for yml_file in sorted(workflows_dir.glob(...

Input

{
  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nworkflows_dir = Path(\".github/workflows\")\nmodified_files = []\n\nfor yml_file in sorted(workflows_dir.glob(\"*.yml\")):\n    with open(yml_file, 'r') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n    \n    original_content = content\n    \n    # Pattern 1: Change branches from lines with main + release/* to just release/2.10\n    # This looks for the exact pattern with proper indentation\n    lines = content.split('\\n')\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(lines):\n        if 'branches:' in lines[i] and (i+1 < len(lines)):\n            # Check if next lines have \"- main\" followed by \"- release/*\"\n            if (i+1 < len(lines) and '- main' in lines[i+1] and \n                i+2 < len(lines) and 'release/*' in lines[i+2]):\n                # Found the pattern, replace it\n                indent = len(lines[i]) - len(lines[i].lstrip())\n                lines[i+1] = ' ' * (indent + 2) + '- release/2.10'\n                # Remove the release/* line\n                lines.pop(i+2)\n                modified_files.append(yml_file.name)\n        i += 1\n    \n    new_content = '\\n'.join(lines)\n    if new_content != original_content:\n        with open(yml_file, 'w') as f:\n            f.write(new_content)\n        print(f\"✓ {yml_file.name}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal files modified: {len(set(modified_files))}\")\nPYEOF\n"
}

  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nworkflows_dir = Path(\".github/workflows\")\nmodified_files = []\n\nfor yml_file in sorted(workflows_dir.glob(\"*.yml\")):\n    with open(yml_file, 'r') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n    \n    original_content = content\n    \n    # Pattern 3: Change \"main\" (without dash) to \"release/2.10\"\n    lines = content.split('\\n')\n    for i in range(len(lines)):\n        # Pattern: branches: followed by \"main\" (no dash)\n        if 'branches:' in lines[i] and i+1 < len(lines):\n            next_line = lines[i+1]\n            # Check if next line is just \"main\" (not \"- main\")\n            if next_line.strip() == 'main':\n                indent = len(next_line) - len(next_line.lstrip())\n                lines[i+1] = ' ' * indent + 'release/2.10'\n                modified_files.append(yml_file.name)\n        # Pattern: branches: [main, ...] or [main] or branches: [main]\n        elif 'branches:' in lines[i]:\n            # Check if this line contains [main\n            if '[main' in lines[i]:\n                # Replace [main with [release/2.10, and remove \", release\"\n                new_line = lines[i]\n                new_line = new_line.replace('[main, release]', '[release/2.10]')\n                new_line = new_line.replace('[main]', '[release/2.10]')\n                if new_line != lines[i]:\n                    lines[i] = new_line\n                    modified_files.append(yml_file.name)\n    \n    new_content = '\\n'.join(lines)\n    if new_content != original_content:\n        with open(yml_file, 'w') as f:\n            f.write(new_content)\n        if yml_file.name not in modified_files:\n            print(f\"✓ {yml_file.name}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"✓ {yml_file.name} (updated)\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nAdditional files modified: {len(set(modified_files))}\")\nPYEOF\n"

39. $ cat > /tmp/update_branches.sh << 'EOF'
#!/bin/bash

# Update workflow files to use release/2.10 instead of main for release branch

# List of files to update from main to release/2.10
for file in .github/workflows/*.yml; do
    if grep -q "branches:" "$file"; then
        # Check if file has both "- main" and "- release/*"
        if grep -A 2 "branches:" "$file" | grep -q "- main" && \
           grep -A 2 "branches:" "$file" | grep -q "- release/\*"; then
            # Update: remove "- main" and "- release/*", add just "- release/2.10"
            sed -i '/branches:/,/^$/{ /- main$/d; /- release\/\*/d; /branches:/a\      - release/2.10
            }' "$file"
            echo "Updated $file"
        fi
    fi
done
EOF
chmod +x /tmp/update_branches.sh

import os
import re
from pathlib import Path

workflows_dir = Path(".github/workflows")
count = 0

for yml_file in workflows_dir.glob("*.yml"):
    with open(yml_file, 'r') as f:
        content = f.read()
    
    original_content = content
    
    # Replace pattern: main and release/* with just release/2.10
    # This pattern handles the case where we have both main and release/*
    pattern = r'(\s+branches:\s*\n\s+- main\s*\n\s+- release/\*)'
    replacement = r'\n      - release/2.10'
    
    if re.search(pattern, content):
        content = re.sub(pattern, replacement, content)
        with open(yml_file, 'w') as f:
            f.write(content)
        print(f"Updated: {yml_file.name}")
        count += 1

print(f"\nTotal files updated: {count}")

[dcp] remove psutil dependency in asyncprocessexecutor for oss (#170121)

[dcp] remove psutil dependency in asyncprocessexecutor for oss (#169985)

Summary: Adding the psutil dependency in oss, caused issues for some flows https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/pull/169613#issuecomment-3633740571. Removing it, as it's not really needed. The number of objects collected by GC is enough information.
